6618 — low lunge yoga pose anjaneyasana i

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 6618 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A yoga low lunge with the back knee down and arms overhead. It opens the hips and stretches the front of the thigh.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Use a balanced standing blocking pose, preserving any source-documented stance, support foot and torso lean. When standing itself is an authored default, review that reversible choice before motion production.

Complete ordered source instructions

6692 — barbell deadlift (side pov)

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 6692 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

The classic barbell deadlift shown from the side to check form. It trains the whole posterior chain.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with the bar over mid-foot and grip it just outside your legs. Brace your core, flatten your back and pull the slack out of the bar.

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: quadruped.

A donkey kickback on a cable that sends the leg back and slightly out. It focuses on the glutes.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Attach an ankle cuff to a low pulley and put it on one ankle. Get on all fours facing the machine.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 6742.json#/instructions/0, 6742.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Attach an ankle cuff to a low pulley and put it on one ankle. 6742.json#/instructions/0
  2. Get on all fours facing the machine. 6742.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keep your core braced and back flat. 6742.json#/instructions/2
  4. Kick the leg back and slightly out on a diagonal. 6742.json#/instructions/3
  5. Squeeze your glute at the top. 6742.json#/instructions/4
  6. Return slowly, then switch legs after your reps. 6742.json#/instructions/5

6744 — resistance band side walk squat

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 6744 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A squat position with side steps against a band. It strengthens the outer hips and thighs.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Use a balanced standing blocking pose, preserving any source-documented stance, support foot and torso lean. When standing itself is an authored default, review that reversible choice before motion production.

authored_from_source_context; source verified: false. Body-pose class uses cited setup/action or title/description context, or an explicitly authored neutral default; detailed placement is a reversible proposal. Basis: source_classification. 6744.json#/name

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

Legs Feet

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 6744.json#/name

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Put a band around your thighs or just above the knees. 6744.json#/instructions/0
  2. Drop into a quarter squat with chest up. 6744.json#/instructions/1
  3. Step sideways, keeping tension on the band. 6744.json#/instructions/2
  4. Bring the trailing foot in without letting the feet touch. 6744.json#/instructions/3
  5. Take several steps one way, then return the other way. 6744.json#/instructions/4
  6. Keep your knees pushed out. 6744.json#/instructions/5

6745 — cable seated foot eversion

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 6745 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A seated ankle move turning the foot outward against a cable. It strengthens the outer ankle and lower leg.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

6747 — kneeling glute press

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 6747 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: quadruped.

A kneeling move pressing one leg back to fire the glute. Great for learning to feel the glutes work.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Get on all fours with hands under shoulders and knees under hips. Brace your core and keep your back flat.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 6747.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Get on all fours with hands under shoulders and knees under hips. 6747.json#/instructions/0
  2. Brace your core and keep your back flat. 6747.json#/instructions/1
  3. Press one foot back and up, leading with the heel. 6747.json#/instructions/2
  4. Squeeze the glute at the top without arching your back. 6747.json#/instructions/3
  5. Lower with control. 6747.json#/instructions/4
  6. Finish the reps, then switch legs. 6747.json#/instructions/5
